Skip blank rows\n            if not any((v or \"\").strip() for v in row.values()):\n                continue\n\n            vendor = (row.get(\"vendor\") or \"\").strip()\n            if not vendor:\n                continue\n\n            acct = (row.get(\"account_number\") or \"\").strip()\n            if acct:\n                if account_number is None:\n                    account_number = acct\n                elif account_number != acct:\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        f\"Multiple account numbers in {csv_path}: {account_number} vs {acct}\"\n                    )\n\n            beginning = _float(row.get(\"beginning_balance\", \"0\"))\n\n            month_net: Dict[str, float] = {}\n            for mkey, _mlabel in MONTHS:\n                adds_key = f\"{mkey}_adds\"\n                amort_key = f\"{mkey}_amortization\"\n                if adds_key in row and amort_key in row:\n                    month_net[mkey] = _float(row.get(adds_key, \"0\")) - _float(\n                        row.get(amort_key, \"0\")\n                    )\n                else:\n                    # Months not present in this CSV are treated as 0 net activity.\n                    month_net[mkey] = 0.0\n\n            items.append(ScheduleItem(vendor=vendor, beginning_balance=beginning, month_net=month_net))\n\n    if account_number is None:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Could not detect account_number from {csv_path}\")\n\n    return items, account_number\n\n\ndef read_ledger_balances(json_path: Path, key: str) -> LedgerBalances:\n    data = json.loads(json_path.read_text())\n    if key not in data:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Ledger JSON missing key '{key}'\")\n\n    raw = data[key]\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"Ledger JSON key '{key}' must be an object\")\n\n    month_ending: Dict[str, float] = {}\n    for mkey, _mlabel in MONTHS:\n        if mkey in raw:\n            month_ending[mkey] = float(raw[mkey])\n\n    if not month_ending:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Ledger JSON key '{key}' had no recognized months\")\n\n    return LedgerBalances(month_ending=month_ending)\n\n\ndef _set_col_widths(ws) -> None:\n    ws.column_dimensions[\"A\"].width = 32\n    ws.column_dimensions[\"B\"].width = 18\n    for col_idx in range(3, 15):  # C..N\n        ws.column_dimensions[get_column_letter(col_idx)].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions[\"O\"].width = 14\n\n\ndef build_detail_sheet(\n    wb: Workbook,\n    sheet_name: str,\n    title: str,\n    items: List[ScheduleItem],\n    ledger: LedgerBalances,\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Create a detail sheet and return control row indices.\"\"\"\n\n    ws = wb.create_sheet(title=sheet_name)\n    _set_col_widths(ws)\n\n    # C..N\n        ws.cell(row=3, column=i).value = mlabel\n    ws[\"O3\"].value = \"Total\"\n\n    for cell in ws[3]:\n        cell.font = Font(bold=True)\n        cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal=\"center\")\n\n    # Line items start at row 6.\n    first_item_row = 6\n    for idx, item in enumerate(items):\n        r = first_item_row + idx\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=1, value=item.vendor)\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=2, value=float(item.beginning_balance))\n\n        for col_offset, (mkey, _mlabel) in enumerate(MONTHS, start=3):\n            ws.cell(row=r, column=col_offset, value=float(item.month_net.get(mkey, 0.0)))\n\n        # Column O: period-ending balance for the line item (beginning + sum of net activity).\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=15).value = f\"=B{r}+SUM(C{r}:N{r})\"\n\n    last_item_row = first_item_row + len(items) - 1\n\n    month_totals_row = last_item_row + 1\n    ending_balance_row = last_item_row + 2\n    variance_row = last_item_row + 3\n    gl_balance_row = last_item_row + 4\n\n    # Control rows labels.\n    ws.cell(row=month_totals_row, column=1, value=\"Month Totals\").font = Font(bold=True)\n    ws.cell(row=ending_balance_row, column=1, value=\"Ending Balance\").font = Font(bold=True)\n    ws.cell(row=variance_row, column=1, value=\"Variance\").font = Font(bold=True)\n    ws.cell(row=gl_balance_row, column=1, value=\"GL Balance\").font = Font(bold=True)\n\n    # GL row is below Variance by contract.\n    for col in range(2, 16):  # B..O\n        col_letter = get_column_letter(col)\n        ws.cell(row=variance_row, column=col).value = (\n            f\"={col_letter}{ending_balance_row}-{col_letter}{gl_balance_row}\"\n        )\n\n    #
